### 2026-10-09 07:29:52  #國際

【中國代表：日本已成為威脅地區和平穩定的突出因素】出席第81屆聯大裁軍與國際安全委員會（聯大一委）的中國代表團團長、外交部軍控司司長孫曉波8日在該委員會一般性辯論發言，指出日本現政權加速“再軍事化”，已成為威脅地區和平穩定的突出因素。孫曉波說，今年是東京審判開庭80週年。當年，日本以承認軍國主義罪行、接受《聯合國憲章》全部內容為前提加入聯合國。但日本現政權否認侵略歷史、美化戰爭罪行、對二戰戰犯頂禮膜拜，同時持續強軍擴武，加速“再軍事化”，已成為威脅地區和平穩定的突出因素。日本現職高官公然發表不設禁忌討論擁有核武器的狂妄言論，企圖拋棄“無核三原則”，這是對國際核不擴散體系的嚴重挑釁和踐踏。日本提出刪除《聯合國憲章》中的“敵國條款”是其披著“國家正常化”外衣挑戰戰後國際秩序的最新例證。日本應該深刻反省侵略歷史，徹底同軍國主義切割，徹底放棄“再軍事化”圖謀，切實履行不擴散核武器的國際法義務。（新華社）

【券業平均淨佣金率降至1.71‱ 經紀業務成最大收入來源】近日，中國證券業協會（簡稱“中證協”）向券商下發了《證券公司2026年上半年度經營情況分析》（下文稱《分析》）。《分析》顯示，上半年，全行業平均代理買賣證券淨佣金率下降至1.71‱，投資者交易成本顯著降低；券業收入結構大變，經紀業務收入超越自營收入，成了第一大收入來源。截至上半年末，外資持股較上年末增長近30%。

### 2026-10-09 07:29:12  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Eases on Trump’s Remarks About Iran — Brent crude fell below $104 per barrel on Friday, trimming gains from the previous session after President Donald Trump said on social media that the US was engaged in “productive discussions” with Iran and would refrain from attacking the country before the midterm elections. Trump also claimed that record volumes of crude were passing through the Strait of Hormuz, while stressing that the US naval blockade of Iranian ports would remain fully in place. However, later reports indicated that the US had prepared plans for three days of strikes against Iran, targeting drone and missile stockpiles, energy infrastructure and other sites. On Thursday, Brent prices surged by as much as 5.7% as Tehran intensified attacks on tankers navigating the Strait of Hormuz, with nine vessels reportedly targeted over the past week. Meanwhile, Hurricane Isaias threatened offshore oil production in the Gulf of Mexico, prompting producers to shut in around 1.3 million barrels per day of crude output.

【北交所三月持有期基金密集發行，低估值下迎來佈局視窗】國慶假期後，首批北交所三個月持有期主題基金進入密集發行期。根據發行安排，富國基金旗下北交所三個月持有期基金募集時間為10月8日至10月21日；華夏基金、匯添富基金相關產品發售時間為10月12日至10月23日；南方、嘉實北交所三個月持有期基金則定於10月12日至10月30日發售。上述基金單隻募集上限均為5億元。在此之前，易方達北交所三個月持有期基金已於9月30日完成募集，並實現超募。華夏基金股票投資部總監顧鑫峰表示，在資金結構改善、估值優勢、回購升溫等因素推動下，當前正是佈局北交所的較好視窗。（21世紀經濟報道）

### 2026-10-09 07:24:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Tanzania #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Tanzania Inflation Rate Holds at 4.3% in September — The annual inflation rate in Tanzania stood at 4.3% in September 2026, unchanged from August and staying at its highest level since April 2023. Upward pressure came primarily from transportation (14.0% vs 13.8% in August); housing and utilities (2.5% vs 2.5%); furnishings and household equipment (2.6% vs 2.6%); education (3.2% vs 3.2%) and information and communication (1.5% vs 1.5%). Meanwhile, price growth eased for food and non-alcoholic beverages (3.9% vs 3.7%), while alcoholic beverages and tobacco inflation slowed to 1.1% from 1.5%. Softer inflation was also recorded for clothing and footwear (1.7% vs 1.8%), health (1.4% vs 1.5%), recreation and culture (0.5% vs 0.7%) and restaurants and accommodation services (2.9% vs 3.0%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose by 0.1% in September, the same pace as in the previous month.

【摩根士丹利警告稱甲骨文專案延期或帶來信用風險】摩根士丹利在報告中表示，如果甲骨文的一些大型專案延期，其資料中心收入的確認可能會推遲，並對這家科技巨頭的債務帶來影響。甲骨文為人工智慧服務建設資料中心的計劃最近遇到了電力供應問題。摩根士丹利分析師援引Aterio的分析稱，甲骨文位於威斯康星州Port Washington的Lighthouse專案包括四座資料中心，面臨“重大風險”，即當地到2028年仍無法獲得所需電力，這將比甲骨文設定的2027年下半年投運目標晚數月。9月，甲骨文向其位於新墨西哥州的Project Jupiter專案開發商發出通知，並援引不可抗力條款，以規避延期成本。媒體週四報道稱，為使專案按計劃推進，公司正考慮在等待天然氣管道投運期間，用卡車向專案運送天然氣。對於一家預計將借貸數百億美元為基礎設施建設籌資的公司而言，任何延期都可能推遲收入和現金流，進而影響其信用狀況。

【美政府暫停多家科企外籍員工綠卡申請 調查9所大學涉簽證問題 】美國聯邦政府8日說，將暫停微軟等多家科技企業參與“永久勞工認證”計劃。該計劃是持有H-1B簽證的外籍員工透過僱主申請美國職業移民永久居留權（綠卡）的第一步。同時，哈佛大學、斯坦福大學等9所美國大學正因“涉嫌J-1簽證欺詐”而面臨調查。美國副總統萬斯當天在白宮舉行的新聞釋出會上宣佈針對微軟的決定時稱，該公司“存在欺詐行為”，微軟必須僱用優秀的美國工人，“你們不能解僱美國工人，然後用條件不穩定的外國工人取而代之”。美國勞工部長桑德林補充說，奧多比、高知特、印孚瑟斯等大型科技公司將被暫停參與為其員工申請綠卡的計劃，“我們將不再接受或處理任何涉及這些公司新的或待處理的永久勞工認證申請”。萬斯說，針對相關科技企業的這一措施將持續到“必要期限”為止。（新華社）

NZX 50 Poised for 2nd Straight Weekly Decline — The NZX 50 dropped 51 points, or 0.4%, to 13,641 in Friday morning trade, touching its lowest level since September 16 and reversing a slight gain in the previous session. The index tracked a decline on Wall Street overnight amid selling in tech stocks and rising oil prices. Rising oil prices continued to pressure sentiment, raising concerns about inflation and interest rate hikes. Traders were also cautious ahead of the release of New Zealand's food inflation and business PMI data next week, as well as US and Chinese inflation data that could provide further clues about economic conditions in the two countries. Financials, industrials, and consumer discretionary stocks mainly weighed on the index, with notable losses from Infratil (-2.2%), Ventia Services Group (-1.6%), Fletcher Building (-1.5%), South Port New Zealand (-1.2%), and Auckland International Airport (-1.1%). For the week, the index was heading for a 0.3% loss, which would mark its second consecutive weekly decline.

### 2026-10-09 04:58:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Uruguay #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Uruguay Central Bank Hikes Policy Rate — Uruguay’s central bank raised its monetary policy rate by 25 bps to 6.0%, aiming to keep inflation expectations anchored and bring inflation toward the 4.5% target over the two-year monetary policy horizon. The bank said international geopolitical tensions continue to push up energy prices and, indirectly, those of other tradable goods, contributing to upward revisions in inflation expectations. Against this backdrop, rates at major central banks and in international financial markets have also moved higher. Domestically, the economy is showing signs of resilience after sector-specific and weather-related shocks temporarily affected activity. The bank said inflation remains on a convergent path toward the target as these temporary effects fade, but warned that persistent geopolitical and climate-related shocks pose significant risks to prices and inflation expectations.

A PoderData/Aya poll showed Senator Flávio Bolsonaro with 53% of valid votes, against 47% for incumbent Lula. In Sunday’s first-round election, Bolsonaro finished ahead of Lula, contrary to most polls, pushing the index to an all-time high on Monday, led by financial stocks. Markets view the senator as more fiscally restrictive amid elevated domestic yields and weak business activity. His economic team is considering spending cuts equivalent to around 2% of GDP. Financials traded mixed, with B3 gaining 2% and Bradesco falling 1.3%. Petrobras followed the sharp rise in international oil prices, gaining 2.1%. Retail stocks also supported the index, with Magazine Luiza jumping 5.6%. Meanwhile, Vale extended its losing streak to four sessions, falling 1.6% as iron ore prices hit an 18-month low in China.

【Anthropic宣佈禁止“虐待”其旗下AI】據報道，Anthropic對其AI使用政策作出調整，以應對新的高風險濫用行為，包括選舉干預、武器開發、監控，以及醫療和金融領域的應用。但其中一項最重要的變化是，禁止對Claude進行“持續且不必要的虐待或殘忍行為”。去年8月，該公司宣佈，作為其“模型福祉”研究的一部分，將允許Claude終止與“持續實施有害或辱罵行為”的使用者的對話。此次更新指出，終止對話仍是“主要執行機制”；對於是否會採取進一步的執行措施（例如可能封禁使用者），Anthropic未予置評。Anthropic表示，新政策更新“僅適用於極端情況，即使用者在毫無明顯目的的情況下反覆對我們的模型實施殘酷行為。該政策不適用於使用者常見的不滿、反駁、黑暗創意主題，以及模型測試和研究等情況。”

The firm said terminating conversations remains the primary enforcement mechanism and declined to comment on whether it will take further measures such as account bans. 【美聯儲穆薩萊姆：未來6至9個月內可能需要進一步加息】美國聖路易斯聯儲主席穆薩萊姆表示，美聯儲需要再次加息，以推動通脹回到2%的目標水平。他稱，為了在“及時”期限內實現通脹目標，貨幣政策需要進一步收緊。穆薩萊姆表示，如果“及時”意味著大約18個月，那麼未來6至9個月內利率可能需要在適當時間進一步上調。他稱，當前通脹仍是美國經濟面臨的主要問題，但經濟增長強勁、就業市場保持穩定，美聯儲可能在不明顯損害就業的情況下壓低通脹。在被問及10月27日至28日議息會議是否應加息時，穆薩萊姆表示自己保持開放態度，尚未預判會議結果，但通脹形勢要求政策制定者繼續考慮進一步收緊政策。穆薩萊姆表示，儘管美債收益率明顯上升，但金融環境仍然寬鬆，並支援經濟增長。他稱，收益率上升並非意味著投資者失去對美聯儲的信心，而是反映市場預計實際利率將上升，以及強勁經濟環境下資本競爭加劇。

### 2026-10-09 04:25:13  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #US Economy #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #S&P500 #Impact bearish

【MUSALEM: Fed may need further hikes in next 6–9 months】St. Louis Fed President MUSALEM said the Fed will likely need additional rate hikes to bring inflation back to 2% and that policy must tighten further to achieve the target within a 'timely' horizon. He said if 'timely' is roughly 18 months, rates may need to be raised again at an appropriate time over the next 6–9 months. Inflation remains the principal US economic problem, he added, but strong growth and a stable labor market suggest the Fed could lower inflation without materially harming employment. Asked about the Oct. 27–28 FOMC meeting, he said he is open and has not pre-judged the outcome, but inflation dynamics require continued consideration of further tightening. Despite a notable rise in US Treasury yields, he judged financial conditions still accommodative and supportive of growth, saying higher yields reflect expected higher real rates and increased capital competition amid a strong economy rather than a loss of confidence in the Fed.

### 2026-10-09 04:15:40  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Crude Gains on Supply Disruptions — Brent crude rose above $103 a barrel in a volatile session on Thursday as Iran ramped up attacks on tankers transiting the Strait of Hormuz and Hurricane Isaias threatened US offshore production in the Gulf of Mexico. Nine tankers have come under attack in and around Hormuz over the past week, jeopardizing the recent recovery in crude oil flows through the strait. About 9.5 million bpd exited Hormuz in the week ended Tuesday, around 30% below normal levels before the Iran war. In the Gulf of Mexico, oil companies have shut in about 500,000 bpd, or 25% of offshore production, as Hurricane Isaias moves toward the Alabama, Mississippi and Florida Panhandle coasts. Prices had earlier retreated from session highs after US President Donald Trump said the US would not attack Iran before the November midterm elections. Earlier reports indicated that Trump and his national security team were considering renewed strikes in the coming weeks.

### 2026-10-09 04:08:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Retreat on Tech Selloff — US stock indices closed lower on Thursday after the Financial Times reported that OpenAI's revenue was sharply below figures previously published by other outlets. The S&P 500 fell 0.5% and the Nasdaq slipped 1.4%, while the Dow closed 52 points higher. The report cited documents indicating annualized revenue of $50 billion rather than the $70 billion previously estimated. This reignited concerns over profitability for LLM developers and the sustainability of their soaring investments in AI infrastructure, which have supported both the stock market and US GDP growth this year. Chipmakers posted sharp losses, with Nvidia down 2.9%, Broadcom shedding 4.6% and Micron losing 4.8%. Hyperscalers also traded lower, with Alphabet falling 0.7%, Microsoft down 1.3% and Oracle tumbling 5.8%. Meanwhile, energy prices were volatile, with oil rebounding as Iran ramped up attacks on tankers transiting the Strait of Hormuz and a hurricane threatened US offshore production in the Gulf of Mexico.

### 2026-10-09 03:19:50  #其他

【Anthropic 推出面向基礎設施與軟體的網路安全計劃】Anthropic 推出“Anthropic 網路安全使命”（Anthropic Cyber Mission），這是一項長期承諾，將利用其工具、研究能力和資源，幫助防禦方保護關鍵基礎設施和開源軟體。在關鍵基礎設施保護方面，推出“關鍵基礎設施防禦計劃”（CIDP），將前沿模型、駐場工程師和威脅研究能力提供給保護電網、水務系統、交通網路和政府系統背後運營技術的防禦方。在開源軟體方面，推出OSS Scanner，免費為開源專案提供由Anthropic最強模型執行的定期安全掃描。CIDP的創始合作伙伴包括埃森哲、博思艾倫、CrowdStrike、德勤、Dragos、日立、Insane Cyber、Nozomi Networks、Palo Alto Networks、普華永道和羅克韋爾自動化。未來幾個月，CIDP將擴充套件至更多合作伙伴和更多行業。

【胡塞武裝稱第三次襲擊沙特機場和空軍基地】葉門胡塞武裝當地時間8日發表宣告稱，當天晚間，胡塞武裝向沙特利雅得哈立德國王國際機場發射兩枚巡航導彈，同時向納季蘭機場發射一枚彈道導彈，並向海米斯穆謝特空軍基地發射一枚彈道導彈。這是胡塞武裝8日第三次發表宣告稱襲擊哈立德國王國際機場。胡塞武裝稱，上述襲擊均“精準命中目標”，導致利雅得和納季蘭兩座機場的航空執行受到影響，並稱機場及海米斯穆謝特空軍基地遭到破壞。胡塞武裝還表示，沙特過去24小時針對胡塞武裝控制區發動了59次空襲，目標涉及葉門首都薩那以及焦夫省、塔伊茲省、馬裡卜省、荷臺達省、哈傑省、薩達省、拉赫季省、賴馬省、伊卜省和貝達省。局勢升級以來沙特針對葉門胡塞武裝控制區的空襲和導彈襲擊已達1857次。 (央視新聞)

### 2026-10-09 01:41:41  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 3

Tech Shares Plunge on Thursday — Tech shares plunged on Thursday after the Financial Times reported that OpenAI's revenue was sharply below figures published by outlets previously. The S&P 500 fell 0.8% and the Nasdaq 100 slipped 1.6%, while the Dow held slightly below the flatline. The report cited documents indicating annualized revenue of $50 billion rather than the $70 billion previously estimated. This reignited concerns over profitability for LLM developers and the sustainability of their soaring investments in AI infrastructure, which have carried both the stock market and US GDP growth this year. Nvidia fell 3%, while Broadcom, SpaceX, AMD, Intel, and Arm dropped between 4% and 8%. Meanwhile, energy prices eased after President Trump signaled the US would not strike Iran before the midterm elections, cooling the rally in long-dated yields. Consumer defensive shares and payment processors rebounded in relief, with Walmart, Visa, and Mastercard adding more than 2%.

### 2026-10-09 01:31:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Softens on Trump Comments — Brent crude oil eased to $103 per barrel on Thursday from $106 earlier in the session, as US President Trump downplayed the prospect of an imminent strike on Iran that would re-escalate the conflict in the Middle East. The US president signaled that discussions with Tehran were productive and that the US would not strike Iran before the midterm elections early next month. Still, crude held firmly above the one-month low touched yesterday, as attacks on laden tankers in the Persian Gulf halted the nascent recovery in exports from GCC members. On top of that, supply disruptions emerged in the Gulf of Mexico, where Chevron and Shell began curbing production and evacuating offshore personnel as Tropical Storm Isaias approached. As of Wednesday, roughly 25.1% of US Gulf oil production and 16.4% of natural gas output had been shut in. Earlier data from the EIA showed that the US Strategic Petroleum Reserve fell further, approaching multi-decade lows.

【美國抵押貸款利率連續七週上漲 加劇住房負擔能力危機】美國抵押貸款利率三年來首次連續七週上升，給本已疲弱的房地產市場帶來更大壓力。房地美週四在一份宣告中表示，30年期固定利率抵押貸款的平均利率從一週前的7.28%升至7.4%。目前該利率為2023年11月以來最高。一年前為6.3%。抵押貸款利率重返7%以上，正在加劇美國住房負擔能力危機。距離中期選舉不到四周，這一問題也日益成為焦點。全美有意購房者正同時受到借貸成本高企以及房價仍接近歷史高位的擠壓。摩根大通分析師週三在給客戶的一份研究報告中寫道：“美國住房負擔能力仍接近全球金融危機以來最差水平，市場實際上已經凍結。保險和房產稅也正在成為另一個住房負擔衝擊，疊加在高房價和高企的抵押貸款利率之上。”

US Mortgage Rates Climb to Highest Since 2023 — The US 30-year fixed-rate mortgage averaged 7.4% as of October 8th, 2026, rising further above 7% to reach its highest level since November 2023 and up from 7.28% in the previous week. This marks the seventh consecutive week with rate increases, as an intensifying selloff in the bond market amid expectations of higher-for-longer interest rates has kept 10-Year Treasury yields at their highest level in decades. Mortgage rates tend to closely track these these sovereign yields, pushing borrowing costs higher for homebuyers. A year ago, the the 30-year fixed-rate mortgage averaged 6.3%. Meanwhile, the 15-year fixed-rate mortgage averaged 6.73%, its highest level since May 2024, up from 6.6% last week.

Tech and industrials were also under pressure, with Infineon down 3.4% and Siemens falling 2.6%. Automakers fell as European buyers continue to shift toward electric vehicles amid higher fuel prices, with Volkswagen down 4.5%. BMW (2.8%) and Mercedes Benz (2%) also fell. On the upside, defense Rheinmetall (0.9%) advanced while British Premier Burnham stated the UK would be open to Germany joining a partnershipto develop a new fighter jet alongside other countries. E.ON rose 0.4% after reporting its successful acquisition of OVO in the UK.

Namibia Inflation Rate Hits Over 2-Year High of 5.4% — Namibia's annual inflation rate rose to 5.4% in September 2026, the highest in over two years, from 5.0% in August. The pickup was driven mainly by transportation, where inflation increased to 14.9% from 13.2% in August, owing to fuels (30.4% vs 25.1%). Prices also advanced faster for health (5.3% vs 5%) and hotels, cafés and restaurants (6.3% vs 6.2%), while food & non-alcoholic beverages held steady at 4%. By contrast, housing & utilities eased (4.2% vs 4.3%) and the decline in communication prices narrowed (-0.9% vs -1.1%). On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.4%, slowing from a 0.6% increase in the prior month.

【ING: ECB September hike widely supported, October hike unlikely】ING economist Carsten Brzeski said ECB minutes from September show the decision to raise rates had broad support, but internal discussion was more balanced than ECB President LAGARDE’s post-meeting remarks implied, making a further hike in October unlikely. Some officials judged the energy shock may be shorter than previously assumed, while others questioned claims of the economy’s resilience to the shock. Continued rises in bond yields have reinforced views among some officials that additional tightening is unnecessary. Brzeski added that, with the bond market effectively tightening financial conditions for the ECB, appetite for further rate increases may be weaker than at the September meeting.

【Poland central bank warns of inflation risk, may raise rates for first time in four years if necessary】Poland central bank governor Adam Glapiński said the bank is prepared to deliver the first rate hike in four years if inflation risks intensify. He added there are currently no signs the recent fuel-price surge is passing through to the broader economy, lowering the likelihood of a November tightening; derivatives-implied odds of a November hike fell after his remarks. The Monetary Policy Council (MPC) led by Glapiński held the policy rate at 3.75% on Wednesday, in line with expectations. A government cap on retail fuel prices is expected to help pull this month’s inflation down from September’s 4% 15-month high.

Natural Gas Halts Rebound Momentum — Natural gas futures fell below $3.18 per MMBtu on Thursday, halting four consecutive sessions of gains as higher domestic storage momentarily offset the outlook for stronger heating demand and persistent supply tightness elsewhere. Data from the EIA showed US energy companies added 85 billion cubic feet to storage in the week ending October 2, exceeding expectations of 79 bcf and rivaling peak injections seen in June. On top of that, domestic supply availability could increase as Gulf hurricanes threaten to disrupt operations at major LNG export terminals. This stalled the steady rally in natural gas futures seen earlier this month, when forecasts of colder weather prompted utilities to position for early-season heating demand. Meanwhile, overseas demand for US LNG remained elevated across Europe and Asia as strikes against commercial vessels in the Strait of Hormuz clouded the export outlook for top producer Qatar.

【US August wholesale inventories revised to +0.5% MoM; sales +1.8% point to firm demand】US Commerce Department (Census Bureau) data: wholesale inventories rose 0.5% MoM in August, revised from a 0.7% preliminary estimate; July inventories rose 1.4% MoM. Inventories were up 6.4% YoY. By category, durables +0.8% MoM, nondurables unchanged, petroleum stocks -3.9%. Wholesale sales climbed 1.8% MoM in August (July +1.0%), signalling accelerating demand. The inventory-to-sales ratio edged down to 1.18 months from 1.19 in July. Firms appear to be rebuilding stocks after five straight quarters of inventory drawdowns despite strong consumer spending and AI investment.

【知情人士：英國與歐盟達成協議 實現碳排放交易體系互通】四名知情談判人士透露，英國與歐盟已成功達成協議，將雙方各自的碳排放交易體系對接。 這項談判自去年啟動，現已收尾，相關協議計劃在未來幾周舉行的英歐峰會上正式公佈。 一名歐盟外交官表示，在官宣之前，雙方目前只是為協議“完成最後的收尾工作”。碳交易市場互通，是英歐雙方在去年峰會上承諾談判的三大議題之一，也是首個順利落地的議題。另外兩項提案的談判仍在推進：一項旨在取消農產品與食品邊境查驗的貿易協定，以及面向年輕人的全新“青年流動簽證”方案。 雙方均表示，三項協議屬於一攬子安排，不會單獨落地。 預計於11月舉辦的本次峰會，既是當前各項談判的截止節點，也有望成為推動雙邊關係進一步走近的跳板。 峰會日期尚未正式公佈，但內部討論的預案是11月20日舉辦。

Dollar Little Changed — The dollar index was little changed at 102.2 on Thursday, hovering near April 2025 highs, as the greenback remained supported by expectations that the Fed will need to keep interest rates elevated for longer. Oil prices moved higher again, adding to inflationary pressures and, in turn, reinforcing expectations of further Fed tightening. Minutes from the Fed’s September meeting showed that most policymakers expect another increase in the federal funds rate this year, although the timing remains uncertain. Fed Governor Waller said on Thursday that additional rate hikes will likely be needed to bring inflation back to target, while emphasizing that there is “flexibility” around the pace of increases. Markets currently price in an around 78% probability that the Fed will leave rates unchanged in October, while the odds of a 25bps hike in December stand at around 69%. Meanwhile, higher government bond yields in Europe continued to weigh on the euro.

【懂車帝：網傳暴力測試是謠言，百公里剎車實驗並非極端工況】10月8日，懂車帝實測曝出尊界V800三次出現剎車踏板支架斷裂，部分網友稱節目內容系極端工況，駕駛員腳踩剎車踏板力度過大。懂車帝有關人員表示，百公里剎車實驗並非極端工況，很多人在高速駕駛場景下都會遇到，是汽車實測最基本的科目之一。本次節目中出現的相關車型制動踏板支架斷裂屬首次出現，且連續三次實驗在同一位置出現相似斷裂現象，存在一定安全隱患。針對三輛測試車剎車踏板支架斷裂時顯示的踏板力，懂車帝方面表示，網傳暴力測試並不存在，而是正常的剎車踏板大力制動。網友關注的“4589N”踏板力數值，實為高速攝像機拍攝實驗畫面時，因攝像機與檢測裝置的液晶屏重新整理幀率不同，拍攝到的液晶屏數字跳動出現殘影，真實峰值資料為1612N，踏板力在達到峰值後迅速下降，但“4589N”被截圖並形成謠言。

### 2026-10-08 22:12:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Wholesale Inventories #Importance 1

US Wholesale Inventories Growth Lower Than Expected — US wholesale inventories rose 0.5% month-on-month to $964.2 billion in August 2026, below the 0.7% in the advance estimate and following a upwardly revised 1.4% increase in July. Despite the slowdown, inventories have increased for seven consecutive months. Nondurable goods inventories were unchanged, as declines in petroleum (-3.9%), groceries (-0.5%), and drugs (-0.8%) offset increases in farm products (6.9%), alcohol (1.7%), paper (0.6%), and apparel (0.6%). Durable goods inventories increased 0.8%, slowing from 1.2% in July, driven by gains in metals (3.1%), machinery (1.3%), and automotive products (0.7%), while computer equipment (-0.8%) and hardware (-0.2%) declined. On a yearly basis, wholesale inventories rose 6.4%, slightly below the previously estimated 6.6%.

【國務院國資委與中國科學院簽署一批重大合作專案】國務院國資委與中國科學院2026年度合作會商會議8日在北京舉行，會上集中籤署一批院企合作協議與所企重大合作專案。國務院國資委主任程福波介紹，自2023年雙方建立年度會商機制以來，院企累計落地合作專案超1200個，投入經費超320億元。三年來，雙方緊扣科技創新與產業創新深度融合要求，在能源開發、深海探測、綠色轉型、裝備製造等領域推進協同攻關，產出一批標誌性成果，為重點領域產業鏈供應鏈安全提供了有力支撐。中央企業研發經費連續四年突破萬億元，累計建成97個原創技術策源地、組建32個創新聯合體，參與全部國家科技重大專項。中國科學院發揮基礎研究與原始創新優勢，在人工智慧、積體電路、生物育種等關鍵領域突破一批卡點堵點，有力支撐載人航天、探月探火、能源安全等國家重大工程實施。（新華社）

Silver Slides to Two-Month Low — Silver prices fell toward $59 an ounce on Thursday, reaching their lowest level since early August, as investors weighed the Federal Reserve’s September meeting minutes for clues on the path for interest rates. The dollar remained near an 18-month high, while US Treasury yields hovered close to their highest levels in more than two decades after the minutes showed that all 19 policymakers supported September’s rate increase, with most favoring another hike before year-end. Markets now see a nearly 17% chance of an October hike and more than an 85% probability of a December increase, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. Meanwhile, geopolitical tensions offered some support to safe-haven demand, with the number of vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz falling to its lowest level in more than two months as fighting in the region intensified, according to shipping data.

Gold Holds Near Two-Month Low — Gold prices hovered around $4,120 an ounce on Thursday, remaining close to a two-month low reached on Wednesday, as investors assessed the Federal Reserve’s September meeting minutes for clues on the interest-rate outlook. The dollar held near an 18-month high, while US Treasury yields remained close to their highest level in more than two decades after the minutes showed all 19 policymakers backed September’s rate hike, with most seeing another increase as appropriate before year-end. Markets now see a near 17% chance of a hike in October and an over 85% probability of a December increase, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. Higher interest rates reduce the appeal of non-yielding gold. Meanwhile, geopolitical tensions continued to support some demand for safe-haven assets. The number of vessels transiting the Strait of Hormuz fell to its lowest level in more than two months as fighting in the region escalated, according to shipping data.

Ibovespa Edges Higher on Petrobras Gains — The Ibovespa edged higher toward 205,000 on Thursday, supported by gains in the energy sector. Petrobras rose nearly 2% as oil prices surged on reports that the US is planning new strikes against Iran, risking the recent rebound in Middle Eastern oil exports. Meanwhile, the financial sector was mixed amid an unfavorable external backdrop and ahead of the first presidential runoff poll following the first round. Global yields also extended their rally as surging oil prices intensified inflationary pressures, while expectations of a hawkish Fed added to pressure on credit-sensitive stocks. Datafolha is due to release the first poll for the presidential runoff following Sunday’s first-round election, in which Flávio Bolsonaro finished ahead of Lula, contrary to most polls. The result pushed the index to an all-time high on Monday, led by financial stocks. On Thursday, Itaú and Bradesco fell about 1% each, while B3 gained 1% and Caixa and BB Seguridade rose nearly 0.5%.

### 2026-10-08 21:30:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 3

US Stocks Pull Back for 2nd Session — US equities fell for a second session on Thursday as Treasury yields rebounded to multi-decade highs and pressured credit conditions for the corporate sector. The S&P 500 fell 0.3%, while the Nasdaq 100 and Dow dropped 0.5%. Oil and fuel prices surged amid reports that the US is planning new strikes against Iran, risking the recent rebound in seaborne oil exports from the Middle East and exacerbating the inflationary outlook for the economy. Minutes from the FOMC's last meeting showed that most members agreed more rate hikes will be necessary to tame inflation, which, combined with widening budget deficits, lifts Treasury yields. AI infrastructure stocks led the losses for a second session due to the sector's exposure to broad risk sentiment and the impact that borrowing costs have on AI capital expenditure. Intel, Lam Research, Marvell, and Micron lost around 2% each. Broadcom fell 1% after issuing $60 billion in debt to help fund Anthropic's buildout.

### 2026-10-08 21:25:02  #公司 #焦點 #國際

【特朗普8月完成500餘筆證券交易：最高2500萬美元買入Meta股票，還購入SpaceX債務產品】
  最新披露的財務申報檔案顯示，美國總統唐納德・特朗普在8月完成500多筆證券交易。其中一筆交易是最高2500萬美元的Meta股票買入；在簽署一項旨在擴大美國商業航天運輸的全面政策的兩天前，他還投資了最高500萬美元的SpaceX債務產品。

媒體對特朗普8月財務披露檔案的分析顯示，這517筆買賣交易的總金額區間約為7430萬美元至2.733億美元。媒體測算，買入金額至少4420萬美元，賣出金額至少3010萬美元。

這份長達18頁的申報檔案顯示，特朗普的投資組合交易依舊十分活躍，不過交易頻次相比6月、7月有所回落，此前兩個月他每月披露的交易數量均超過1000筆。

最大一筆交易發生在8月21日，當日特朗普購入價值500萬至2500萬美元的Meta股票，當天他還對投資組合進行了大範圍調倉。申報顯示，他在同一天分別買入了美國電話電報公司（AT&T）、康菲石油、雅培實驗室、奈飛以及雪佛龍的股票，每筆買入金額區間均為100萬至500萬美元。

在賣出端，8月21日特朗普拋售了超威半導體、丘奇&德懷特（Church&Dwight）的股票，每筆賣出金額在100萬至500萬美元；同時減持波音、家得寶、T-Mobile、Datadog、戴爾科技、帕洛阿爾託網路以及英偉達等公司的股份，每筆減持金額區間為50.001萬至100萬美元。

【湖南臨澧“6·16”煙花爆炸事故調查報告公佈】2025年6月16日，湖南常德市臨澧縣湖南省山洲煙花有限責任公司發生爆炸事故，造成9人死亡、26人受傷、直接經濟損失 2319萬元。2026年10月8日，湖南省人民政府事故調查組公佈事故調查報告。經調查認定：常德臨澧山洲煙花公司“6·16”爆炸事故是一起因涉事企業違規使用電動三輪車運輸亮珠翻車引發爆炸，以及搶工期趕進度、違章冒險作業、“三超一改”、分包轉包，中介機構出具虛假安全評價報告，有關黨委政府和部門安全生產責任不落實、監管不力，導致的較大生產安全責任事故。事故發生後，湖南省紀委監委成立追責問責審查調查組，依規依紀依法對省應急管理廳、常德市應急管理局、常德市工業和資訊化局、臨澧縣委縣政府、臨澧縣應急管理局、臨澧縣停弦渡鎮、瀏陽市應急管理局等單位存在失職失責問題的公職人員嚴肅追責問責。

### 2026-10-08 21:02:11  #國際

【世貿組織上調2026年全球貨物貿易增長預期 】世界貿易組織8日釋出最新全球貿易展望報告說，由於供應鏈調整以及人工智慧領域投資強勁，預計2026年全球貨物貿易將增長3.9%，高於3月預測的1.9%，2027年有望增長4.1%。報告同時顯示，由於中東戰事對運輸和國際旅行帶來影響，2026年全球服務貿易預計增長3.3%，低於3月預測的4.8%，2027年將增長6.4%。世貿組織總幹事伊維拉表示，面對衝擊，一體化的世界經濟和基於規則的貿易體系為各經濟體提供了靈活性，使必需品能夠持續流向需要它們的企業和家庭。但她同時指出，並非所有經濟體都能把握人工智慧等新機遇，因此必須確保基於規則的貿易體系能夠繼續抵禦衝擊、彌合差距，使機遇能夠得到共享。報告顯示，今年上半年，全球貨物貿易增長3.5%，表現超出預期，表明供應鏈有能力應對中東戰事對能源、化肥和運輸市場造成的衝擊。與此同時，人工智慧相關需求強勁，半導體和伺服器等人工智慧相關商品貿易同比增長67%，增速較2024年和2025年進一步加快。（新華社）

US Initial Jobless Claims at Over 2-Month Low — The number of people claiming unemployment benefits fell by 2,000 to 197,000 in the week ending October 3, 2026, coming in below market expectations of 200,000 and marking the lowest initial claim count since the 60-year low of 189,000 in mid-July. Continuing claims, a gauge of ongoing unemployment in the US, rose by 17,000 to 1,716,000, remaining relatively close to the more-than-three-year low touched in the previous week. The data extended a period of resilience in the US labor market as the Federal Reserve continues to cite full employment. Meanwhile, initial claims filed by federal employees, which remain in the spotlight due to the administration's push to shrink the public sector, rose by 26 to 395, remaining well below historical levels.

### 2026-10-08 20:29:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Romania Holds Interest Rate in October — The National Bank of Romania held its policy rate at 6.5% at its October 2026 meeting, unchanged since August 2024, but implied that uncertainty surrounding the global economic outlook could trigger inflationary pressures. The central bank noted that the recent disinflationary trend in the Romanian economy has persisted, even as the removal of electricity price caps and VAT changes on selected goods triggered base effects. Meanwhile, the surge in oil and gas prices due to the war in the Middle East remained a primary source of inflation risk. Still, evidence of slower economic activity—particularly in household consumption and exports from major industries—prevented a clear hawkish tilt in policy messaging. On top of that, the latest data reflected a slight drop in employment levels.

ECB Keeps Rate Path Open Amid Inflation Uncertainty: Minutes — ECB policymakers saw inflation risks skewed to the upside when they delivered their first rate hike since 2023 at the September meeting, but stressed the importance of not signaling any further policy moves and keeping all options open, according to the meeting accounts released Thursday. Officials warned that eurozone inflation could come in higher than projected, but stressed the importance of avoiding guidance on the future interest-rate path given the high uncertainty surrounding the inflation outlook and range of possible scenarios due to the ongoing fighting between the US and Iran, as well as Russia’s war in Ukraine. The ECB also noted that while the policy response should remain proportionate, the 2.50% deposit rate remained within staff estimates of the neutral range.

### 2026-10-08 20:14:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

Mexico Inflation Rate Picks Up as Expected — Mexico’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.45% in September 2026, the highest level since May, up from 3.26% in August. The reading was broadly in line with market expectations but moved further above Banxico’s 3% target. Energy inflation accelerated sharply to 1.48% from 0.81%, while price growth also increased for transportation (3.51% vs. 3.43%) and housing and utilities (3.33% vs. 3.06%). Food inflation rose to 2.55% from 1.49%, its highest level since May, adding further upward pressure. In contrast, inflation for restaurants and hotels slowed to 6.19% from 6.27%. Core inflation also eased further to 3.75% from 3.88%. From the previous month, consumer prices rose 0.42%, accelerating from a 0.20% increase in August.

### 2026-10-08 19:40:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Angola #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Angola Inflation Rate Slows Again in September — Angola’s annual inflation rate eased to 8.43% in September 2026 from 8.78% in August, marking its lowest level since April 2015 and extending the downward trend observed since mid-2024. The moderation was mainly supported by the relative stability of the kwanza and improved domestic supply of essential goods. Inflation slowed for food and non-alcoholic beverages (9.54% vs. 9.94%), transportation (3.32% vs. 3.67%), education (18.53% vs. 19.33%), furnishings (6.02% vs 6.42%) and recreation, sports and culture (3.41% vs 4.18%). In contrast, price growth accelerated for housing and utilities (9.07% vs. 8.24%) and alcoholic beverages and tobacco (9.10% vs. 9.02%). From the previous month, consumer prices rose 0.68%, accelerating from a 0.59% rise in August.

Serbia Holds Interest Rate Unchanged — The National Bank of Serbia held its key policy rate unchanged at 5.75% after its October 2026 meeting, where they have stood since September 2024, but emphasized that upside inflation risks warrant close monitoring. Swiss Franc Weakens Amid Diverging Rate Outlooks — The Swiss franc weakened to 0.83 per USD, its lowest level in a week, remaining under pressure from short positions amid expectations of further monetary tightening across other major economies. Contrasting with other central bank's, the Swiss National Bank kept its key rate unchanged at 0% at its September meeting, while Vice Chairman Martin said no adjustment was currently needed, citing low and stable inflation and limited spillover risks, as the Swiss economy was working "very well". Policymakers have also scaled back threats of currency intervention. Swiss borrowing costs have remained at the world’s lowest for over a year, increasing the it’s appeal as a funding source for carry trades as interest-rate differentials with other major economies widen. As traders take short positions, the selling pressure of franc-loans weakens the currency. However, safe-haven demand amid concerns over debt sustainability in its European peers provides some support, posing a risk to carry trades.

### 2026-10-08 16:27:15  #其他

【雲南鎮雄大營煤礦“12·24”較大煤與瓦斯突出事故調查報告公佈，40名責任人被追責問責】日前，國家礦山安全監察局雲南局公佈《昭通鎮雄大營煤礦有限公司“12·24”較大煤與瓦斯突出事故調查報告》。經調查認定，該事故是一起因企業主體責任落實不到位、地方煤礦安全監管不力導致的生產安全責任事故。2025年12月24日20時01分許，昭通市鎮雄縣大營煤礦有限公司大營煤礦（以下簡稱大營煤礦）發生一起煤與瓦斯突出事故，造成7人遇難，直接經濟損失1596.2萬元。透過現場勘查、調查取證、查閱資料、綜合分析，查明瞭事故發生的經過、原因、人員傷亡、直接經濟損失，認定了事故性質和責任，依法依規提出了對有關責任人員和責任單位的處理建議，並針對事故原因及暴露出的突出問題，提出了事故防範和整改措施。該事故地點位於1503運輸巷掘進工作面迎頭。事故發生後，昭通市人民政府及時啟動應急預案，成立應急救援指揮部，全面開展現場救援工作。經過全力救援，12月27日15時32分，7名遇難人員全部找到，救援結束。經技術鑑定，本次煤與瓦斯突出事故突出煤巖量為591噸，突出瓦斯總量為55300立方米。調查認為，事故發生後，井下作業人員積極開展自救互救，大營煤礦進行前期應急處置，但未在第一時間召請專（兼）職礦山救護隊，兼職礦山救護隊部分救援裝備失效、集結用時57分鐘，存在應急響應不及時、應急準備不充分等問題。調查認定，大營煤礦“12·24”較大煤與瓦斯突出事故是一起因企業主體責任落實不到位，地方煤礦安全監管不力，煤礦出現瓦斯動力現象後未按照突出煤層管理，在未消除突出危險的情況下，掘進作業造成的生產安全責任事故。事故調查組對40名責任人員提出追責問責建議。其中，大營煤礦法定代表人、礦長等5人移交司法機關依法處理；大營煤礦安全副礦長、生產副礦長等11人給予暫停或吊銷安全生產知識和管理能力考核合格證、行政罰款等處罰；東煌煤業公司法定代表人、總經理2人給予行政罰款；煤礦安全生產技術服務機構2人給予行政罰款；昭通市能源局、鎮雄縣等20名事故相關責任人給予黨紀政務處分及組織處理。對大營煤礦給予行政罰款，暫扣煤礦安全生產許可證，責令停產整頓，並進行智慧化改造。對東煌煤業公司給予行政罰款。對煤礦安全生產技術服務機構給予行政罰款並責令停業整頓。責令鎮雄縣能源局、昭通市能源局、鎮雄縣委、縣人民政府作出書面檢查。（央視新聞）

Czechia Unemployment Rate Steady at 5% — The Czech Republic’s unemployment rate remained steady at 5% in September 2026, matching the rate recorded in each of the previous two months. The number of unemployed decreased by 5,514 month-on-month but increased by 30,452 year-on-year to 368,377. Women accounted for 54% of the total unemployed, while men accounted for 46%. Meanwhile, 62,099 applicants left the records, 22,691 more than in the previous month and 11,107 more than in September 2025. A total of 45,568 applicants found new jobs during the month. At the end of September, employment offices registered 24,446 school graduates and young people, accounting for 6.6% of total unemployment, up from 4.8% in August but down from 6.9% a year earlier. Job vacancies rose by 5,085 to 104,359.

The U.S. Bureau of Ocean Energy Management said by Wednesday morning about 511,619 b/d of Gulf of Mexico oil production—roughly 25.08% of the region’s current output—was shut in; eight production platforms and two drilling rigs had been evacuated and one rig moved out of the storm track. ### 2026-10-08 16:15:21  #MKT News #EURUSD #Impact bullish #DXY #Impact bearish

【Dolenc: ECB rate policy can prevent energy price shock from spreading】ECB Executive Board member Dolenc said interest-rate policy can prevent a surge in energy prices from spilling into broader, persistent inflation. He noted monetary policy cannot directly affect energy shocks, geopolitics or structural competitiveness, but can stop an initial price shock from becoming more widespread and long-lasting. The ECB has raised rates twice and is expected to hike again, most likely at the December meeting. September inflation rose to 3.8%, near twice the ECB’s 2% target; Dolenc said roughly 3% inflation is likely to persist through year-end and, absent new shocks, inflation should gradually return to 2% over the next two years while economic growth accelerates.

【《煙花爆竹經營許可實施辦法（修訂徵求意見稿）》公開徵求意見】應急管理部就《煙花爆竹經營許可實施辦法（修訂徵求意見稿）》公開徵求意見。修訂的主要內容：一是嚴格許可程式要求，織牢制度“籬笆”。針對當前個別地區將經營許可許可權下放導致大量不符合安全生產條件的經營單位獲得許可等突出問題，明確“發證機關不得將本級負責的煙花爆竹經營許可證的審批、頒發工作委託下級發證機關實施”；為強化許可把關，刪除選擇性開展現場核查的要求，明確提出對所有批發企業和零售店（點）均應指派2名以上工作人員進行現場核查的要求；依據行政許可等法律法規，縮短了許可時限要求。二是細化經營布點要求，緩解供需矛盾。三是完善批發許可制度，強化源頭管控。四是最佳化零售許可制度，防範遏制安全事故。五是完善監管人員監督管理要求，規範許可行為。六是提高處罰標準，增強對違法違規行為的打擊力度。

### 2026-10-08 16:04:29  #MKT News #Important #Market Themes #Chip Wars #CSI300 #Impact bearish #SZSE Component

【20% limit-down; Yuanjie Technology says operations normal】Oct 8 — China's A-share optical‑chip sector weakened, with multiple stocks plunging. Changguang Huaxin and Yuanjie Technology hit 20% limit-downs, Dongshan Precision hit a 10% limit-down and Sijia Photonics fell over 17%. Market concern followed an overseas report suggesting potential restrictions on optical chips; market sources said implementation is highly uncertain and likely containable. Morgan Stanley's Oct. 1 note drew solely on exchanges between its strategy team and a Washington legal team and repeatedly used hypothetical language (potential, likely, could); it contained no FCC rule text, no proposal discussion record and no official release, and should be treated as a policy conjecture. Yuanjie said recent stock volatility was driven by the Morgan Stanley report, that the sector decline was not company-specific, it would not comment on the report, and that production and operations are normal.

### 2026-10-08 16:03:43  #公司 #市場 #觀點 #焦點

【“20cm”跌停，源傑科技回應：經營正常】10月8日，A股光晶片概念板塊走弱，多隻個股大幅下挫。截至收盤，長光華芯、源傑科技20%幅度跌停，東山精密10%幅度跌停，仕佳光子跌逾17%。近期，境外一則光晶片相關限制令的訊息迅速引起市場關注。多位業內人士分析認為，相關限制落地具有極強不確定性，影響相對可控。從資訊來源看，摩根士丹利10月1日報告的全部資訊來源，是其策略團隊與一支華盛頓法律團隊的交流——報告通篇使用的是potential、likely、could（“潛在”“可能”“或將”）這類推演性措辭，既無FCC任何正式規則文字，也無提案討論記錄，更無官方釋出。換言之，整份報告是一個政策猜想。對於股價大幅下跌，源傑科技表示，近期光晶片企業股價波動受摩根士丹利報告影響，行業整體下跌並非公司個例，公司對該報告不予置評。從公司層面看，目前生產經營正常，各項業務正常開展。（上海證券報）

【高盛：小馬智行-W持續擴張Robotaxi車隊 維持“買入”評級】高盛釋出研報稱，小馬智行-W(02026)持續在中國及海外市場擴張其Robotaxi車隊，並維持今年底規模達3,500輛的目標;該行維持小馬智行“買入”評級。該行指出，小馬智行近期已在多哈開展Robotaxi商業營運，目標邁向全無人駕駛營運，並在克羅埃西亞首都薩格勒布進行全無人駕駛Robotaxi試乘。憑藉大規模量產能力、自動駕駛技術商業化及累積經驗，高盛認為小馬智行的業務已從中國主要一線城市擴充套件至更廣泛地區。小馬智行上月與廣汽商用車合作，基於廣汽T9純電動重卡平臺推出第四代Robotruck，高盛認為新一代Robotruck的自動駕駛套件(ADK)物料成本較上一代降低70%，有助降低每公里運輸成本，對該較高價效比新平臺支援Robotruck商業化持正面看法。

FTSE MIB Hits More Than 4-Month Low — The FTSE MIB slid below 50,000 on Thursday, hitting its lowest level since May, as rising geopolitical tensions compounded fresh M&A uncertainty in the country’s banking sector. Reports showed that the White House asked the Pentagon to prepare strike options against Iran ahead of the midterm elections, pushing oil prices and bond yields higher again. Domestically, major MPS shareholder Francesco Gaetano Caltagirone said his holding company would oppose Monte dei Paschi di Siena’s plans to acquire Banco BPM and Banca Generali, dealing another blow to the lender’s efforts to fend off Intesa Sanpaolo’s takeover bid. Heavyweight financials bore the brunt, led by Mediobanca (-3.6%), MPS (-2.6%), UniCredit (-2.9%), Intesa Sanpaolo (-2.5%) and Banco BPM (-1.5%). Elsewhere, technology stocks also lagged, with STMicroelectronics down 3.3%, Prysmian down 1.7% and Telecom Italia falling 2.4%.

French Stocks Extend Fall to Over 6-Month Low — The CAC 40 fell 1.1% to 7,687 on Thursday, extending losses from the previous session and hitting its lowest level since March as escalating Middle East tensions weighed on risk sentiment. Reports that the White House had asked the Pentagon to prepare strike options against Iran ahead of the midterm elections lifted oil prices and pushed bond yields higher. Bank of France Governor and ECB Governing Council member Emmanuel Moulin warned that the Middle East crisis is affecting economies globally through its impact on inflation and bond markets. His comments came as investors remained wary on France’s fiscal challenges, including efforts to pass a budget and reduce the country's large deficit. Most stocks traded lower, led by L’Oréal (-1.0%), LVMH (-1.1%), Airbus (-1.5%), and Safran (-2.2%). Financial stocks also remained under pressure, particularly BNP Paribas (-2.3%), Crédit Agricole (-1.6%), and Société Générale (-2.9%).

【Analyst: FX volatility rises, carry-trade risk increases】Analyst Jeremy Boulton says rising market volatility has widened FX swings and left carry trades increasingly fragile; adverse moves can erase interest-differential gains and inflict larger losses. Since Fed September-hike prospects firmed, popular carry currencies have moved sharply: Mexican peso down as much as 9%, South African rand down more than 5%, Hungarian forint down over 7%. Japanese interventions have nudged the yen higher, while a French bond-market rout dented confidence, weighing on the euro and lifting the Swiss franc. The backdrop raises downside risk for carry positions; a further equity pullback could trigger a collapse. With investors taking profits into year-end and uncertainty ahead of the November US election, risk assets may face additional pressure.

Heating Oil Rises Toward Record — US heating oil prices rose toward $4.80 per gallon, moving close to their recent peak of $5.20 reached in mid-September, driven by persistent supply concerns. The potential for further escalation between the US and Iran and continued attacks in the Middle East raised concerns over energy flows despite increased regional shipments. Meanwhile, disruptions to Russia’s refining operations and extended diesel-export restrictions, along with China’s reported suspension of October fuel exports, added to global diesel supply constraints. Hurricane Isaias in the Gulf of Mexico also threatened to disrupt oil and refining operations, with Gulf Coast refineries accounting for about half of US refining capacity of 18.2 million bpd. Against this backdrop, EIA data showed distillate stockpiles, which include diesel and heating oil, fell by 42 thousand barrels in the week ended October 2, leaving inventories 12% below the five-year average.

【中方如何評價當前中俄關系？外交部回應】10月8日，外交部發言人毛寧主持例行記者會。 有記者提問，俄羅斯總統助理、海事委員會主席帕特魯舍夫正在中國訪問。能否介紹帕特魯舍夫此次訪問有關安排？中方如何評價當前中俄關系？ 毛寧表示，中俄是新時代全面戰略協作夥伴。近年來，在習近平主席和普京總統的戰略引領下，中俄兩國政治互信不斷深化，戰略協作愈發緊密，互利合作持續拓展，樹立了新型國際關係和相鄰大國關係的典範。 毛寧說，這是帕特魯舍夫先生首次以俄羅斯海事委員會主席的身份訪華。中共中央政治局委員、中央外辦主任王毅以及其他相關部門的負責人，將分別和帕特魯舍夫主席舉行會見，就雙邊關係和涉海合作交換意見。中方願意以此訪為契機，和俄方進一步深化戰略溝通，推動務實合作，為新時代中俄關系不斷注入新的發展動力。 （北京日報）

Gasoline Futures Rise — US gasoline futures rose above $3.30 per gallon, recovering from the previous session’s losses, driven by persistent supply concerns. The potential for further escalation between the US and Iran, along with continued attacks on tankers in the Middle East, kept supply risks elevated despite improving regional shipments. Additionally, Hurricane Isaias threatened to disrupt refinery operations, with Gulf Coast refineries accounting for about half of US refining capacity of 18.2 million bpd. Russia’s restrictions on diesel exports and refinery disruptions, alongside China’s reported suspension of some October fuel exports, also tightened fuel markets. Offering some relief, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories unexpectedly rose by 382 thousand barrels in the week ending October 2, rebounding after two consecutive draws. The accelerated release of remaining IEA emergency oil stocks could also ease some supply pressures.

【Rupee nears record low, tests RBI's remaining defenses】Despite using almost all tools — including rate hikes, measures to attract overseas deposits and tens of billions of dollars of FX intervention — the rupee is closing in on its record low. It sits only about 0.2% from the May trough of 96.97 per dollar, highlighting pressure from a global surge in yields and concerns over high oil prices. RBI intervention has drained FX reserves by $51 billion in the four weeks to Oct. 2, and the first rate increase in nearly four years has not stopped the currency's slide. Ritesh Bhansali, deputy CEO at Mecklai Financial Services Pvt., said the sharp reserve fall is worrying and that intervention is currently the only support for the rupee.

【China urges Japan to punish embassy intruder】China's Foreign Ministry spokesperson Mao Ning, at a routine briefing, commented on reports that on Oct. 1 the Tokyo District Court held a first public hearing for Murata Akihiro, who illegally entered the Chinese embassy in Tokyo. Mao said an active-duty Japan Self-Defense Forces officer entered the embassy armed with a knife and issued violent threats to Chinese diplomatic staff, calling the incident an unprecedented, serious breach of the Vienna Convention on Diplomatic Relations that gravely endangered personnel and embassy premises. She said the episode exposed deep-seated problems, including the rise of right-wing forces and lax discipline among SDF personnel, and that whether Japan handles the case lawfully will test its fulfillment of international obligations and affect its national credibility and international image. China again urged Japan to severely punish the perpetrator as a deterrent.

### 2026-10-08 15:24:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Czech Retail Sales Growth Slows in August — Retail sales in the Czech Republic rose by 4.3% year-on-year in August 2026, slightly above market expectations for a 4.2% gain but softer than the previous month’s 5.1% increase. The slowdown was largely driven by lower sales in automotive fuel (-3.2% vs 5.8% in July). However, turnover growth was still broad-based, led by non-food goods, which climbed 7.4% (vs 6.5%), while food sales stood at 3.0%. Among non-food categories, clothing and footwear sales rose 10.0% (vs 8.4%), contributing the most to overall retail growth, while online and mail-order sales jumped 15.0% (vs 12.4%), marking the strongest increase among retailers. Revenues in non-specialized stores with a predominance of food also increased 3.1% (vs 2.9%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail sales excluding motor vehicles rose 0.2% in August, easing from a slightly upwardly revised 0.7% gain in the preceding period.

Indonesia Motorbike Sales Growth Hits 4-Month High — Motorbike sales in Indonesia surged 15.37% year-on-year to 654,343 units in September 2026, with the number of units sold reaching the highest level since October 2014. It was the strongest growth in motorbike sales since May, accelerating sharply from a 3.18% rise in the previous month. For the first nine months of the year, motorbike sales were 3.71% higher than in the same period last year, reaching 5.02 million units. The Association of Indonesian Motorcycle Industry (AISI) expects sales to remain relatively stable at around 6.4 million to 6.7 million units this year. Last year, motorbike sales grew 1.3% to 6.4 million units, within the target range of 6.4 million to 6.7 million units set by AISI. However, the growth rate was slightly slower than the 1.5% increase recorded in 2024. On a monthly basis, motorbike sales rose 9.7% in September, rebounding from a 6.2% fall in August.

### 2026-10-08 15:17:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Austria Trade Balance Swings to Surplus — Austria recorded a trade surplus of EUR 268.0 million in July 2026, shifting from a deficit of EUR 627.0 million in the same month a year earlier. This marked the largest surplus since February, as export growth outpaced imports. Exports jumped 6.7% year-on-year to EUR 17.6 million, driven largely by increased shipments to both intra-EU and extra-EU countries, which rose 4.8% and 10.8%, respectively. Export growth was supported by machinery and vehicles (+8.2%) and chemical products (+15.9%), offsetting a decline in other goods (-35.1%). Meanwhile, imports grew 1.9% to EUR 17.3 million, lifted by higher purchases from extra-EU countries (+8.9%), while intra-EU countries (-1.4%) recorded declines. Import growth was strongest in machinery and vehicles (+2.4%) and chemical products (+4.4%). In the January–July period, the trade balance recorded a deficit of EUR 3.9 million, as both imports and exports rose at the same pace of 5.7%.

UK Natural Gas Prices Rise to Over 3-Week High — UK natural gas prices climbed above 198 pence per therm, their highest level in more than three weeks, as continued tanker attacks in the Strait of Hormuz heightened supply concerns. Iran has sharply increased attacks on commercial and energy vessels transiting the waterway, underscoring persistent shipping risks and threatening to disrupt the broader recovery in fuel deliveries. Adding to the upward pressure, forecasts point to cooler weather across Northwestern Europe, which could boost heating demand and increase gas consumption. This weather-driven support comes despite steady additions to European gas storage reserves, with facilities now around 73% full, although inventories remain below levels seen at the same point last year. Meanwhile, the surge in Britain’s wind generation in September has reduced its reliance on gas-fired power generation, but this was not enough to insulate the country from the broader price pressures stemming from ongoing disruptions to Gulf supply.

### 2026-10-08 15:09:43  #MKT News

【TrendForce: Cost pressure shifts to demand; 2027 laptop market may face dual hit to shipments and prices】TrendForce said rising component costs are shifting the 2027 laptop-market risk from supply limits to demand weakness. It currently forecasts global laptop shipments will fall by low single digits in 2027; but if DRAM and CPU prices remain elevated and brands pass costs to consumers, higher retail prices could extend replacement cycles and push the annual shipment decline toward high single digits. For a mainstream $900 MSRP model, CPU, DRAM and SSD together made up about 68% of the laptop BOM in 3Q26, highlighting the growing pricing influence of core components. Brands face a triage: raise retail prices and risk demand erosion, absorb costs and compress gross margins, or cut storage/memory specs and weaken competitiveness. TrendForce expects brands will struggle to simultaneously preserve price, specs and margin, making the degree of cost pass-through a key variable for 2027 demand.

【機構：成本壓力轉向需求端，2027年筆記本市場或面臨出貨、價格雙重影響】根據TrendForce集邦諮詢最新筆記本產業研究，2027年筆記本市場的主要風險將由供應限制轉向成本上升對需求的影響。TrendForce集邦諮詢目前預期2027年全球筆記本出貨量將呈低個位數衰退，但若DRAM與CPU價格維持高位，品牌被迫擴大成本轉嫁，導致終端售價上漲，可能進一步延長消費者換機週期，全年出貨衰退幅度不排除加劇至高個位數。2026年由於CPU供給改善、品牌提前採購及部分換機需求提前釋放，支撐筆記本市場出貨，但不代表終端需求實質復甦。隨著部分原先預期於下半年及後續釋出的需求提前反映，後續市場換機動能將受到壓縮，加上低成本庫存逐步消化，品牌將更直接面對CPU、DRAM與SSD價格上升的壓力。

【Commerzbank: Reserve Bank of India hikes 25bp; near-term rate cut off the table】Commerzbank analyst Charlie Lay says the Reserve Bank of India has restarted a tightening cycle, hiking the repo rate 25bp to 5.50% and shifting to an “orderly tightening” stance. The move is precautionary to restrain rising inflation expectations while the economy remains resilient. Lay expects the hike to provide near-term support to the rupee, but notes oil prices, US Treasury yields, the dollar and portfolio flows will remain the dominant drivers of INR direction. Commerzbank says short-term rate cuts are no longer a policy option; India’s CPI forecast for FY2026/27 was raised from 5.0% to 5.2%, and Q4 2026 inflation is expected to reach 6.0%, at the top of the RBI’s 2%–6% target band.

### 2026-10-08 15:01:22  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #US10Y #Impact bearish

【Barclays: High U.S. Treasury yields do not imply cheap valuations】Barclays strategists Demi Hu and Anshul Pradhan say high U.S. Treasury yields do not necessarily mean Treasuries are cheap. If higher yields mainly reflect market expectations for short-term rates, investors earn less extra compensation for holding longer-duration instruments versus rolling into shorter maturities. They note a higher term premium raises compensation but also reflects greater uncertainty and duration risk. A reassessment of near-term monetary policy would mainly affect the front and mid-curve; a sustained rise in the term premium or in the long-run neutral rate would place greater pressure on the long end.

【Danske Bank: Oil market reheats as transport disruptions and geopolitical risk rise】Danske Bank research team says Strait of Hormuz traffic fell to its lowest in over two months. Kpler data show that after last week’s tanker attacks only seven commodity carriers transited the strait on Tuesday, marking the most active week of attacks on tankers since the outbreak of the Iran-related conflict. European diesel prices surged. IEA member countries agreed to accelerate the release of the 400 mln-barrel stockpile pledged in March but will not exceed that amount, effectively front-loading existing commitments rather than adding new supply, a move markets found disappointing. US consideration of a military strike on Iran ahead of the midterm elections further pushed oil above $102/bbl.

### 2026-10-08 14:37:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Finnish Trade Gap Largest in 4 Years — Finland posted a trade deficit of EUR 1.15 billion in August 2026, widening from EUR 0.81 billion in the same month of the previous year, preliminary estimates showed. This marked the largest trade gap since August 2022, as exports rose less than imports. Exports grew 21.8% year-on-year to EUR 6.33 billion, as sales surged to non-EU countries (+49.3%) and EU markets (+4.5%). Sales to the US (+224.3%), Thailand (+74.9%), Switzerland (+66.0%), Australia (+33.7%), and Turkey (+29.4%) saw the largest increases. Meanwhile, imports rose faster, increasing 24.5% to EUR 7.47 billion. It should be noted that imports from EU countries are now compiled using a new methodology starting January 2026, so only total values are comparable with those of 2025. In the first eight months of 2026, the country’s trade shortfall expanded sharply to EUR 2.80 billion from EUR 0.19 billion in the same period a year ago.

Iron Ore Hits 2-Year Low — Iron ore futures dropped below CNY 700 per ton, reaching their lowest level since September 2024 as Chinese markets reopened after a week-long holiday, with weakening steel margins putting further pressure on prices. Industry data showed that just 6.9% of Chinese steelmakers were profitable by the end of September, while average daily hot metal output, a key indicator of iron ore demand, fell to a six-month low of 2.34 million tons. Reports also indicated that some Chinese steelmakers had begun or were planning equipment maintenance amid mounting losses, hurting the outlook for demand for raw materials such as iron ore. In other news, Australian iron ore miner Fortescue reported lower sales as a prolonged standoff with state-backed buyer China Mineral Resources Group over long-term supply agreements and pricing negotiations continued to weigh on activity.

【以色列重申以色列剋制時代已結束】宣告表示，英國工黨政府自2024年7月上臺以來，採取了多項針對以色列的單方面舉措。英方近期針對約旦河西岸猶太人定居點實施制裁，擴大了暫停對以國防出口許可的範圍，對以色列部分部長實施制裁、單方面承認巴勒斯坦國、恢復對聯合國近東救濟工程處的資助，並凍結英以自由貿易協定談判和軍事合作。 作為反制，以方此前已驅逐在加沙國際救援中心工作的英國代表，叫停了英方在約旦河西岸培訓巴勒斯坦安全部隊的活動，禁止12名涉嫌參與“反以活動”並否定以色列生存權的英國民意代表及公民入境；正式終止英國駐耶路撒冷領事館的外交職能並摘除其徽標。 不過宣告宣稱，雖然英國駐耶路撒冷領事館於當天正式關閉，但根據雙方7日達成的諒解協議，以方出於大局考慮，並考慮到該領事館建築屬於英國政府財產，允許英方在原領事館建築內保留一個由7名外交官組成的工作組，在受到嚴格限制的低限度範圍內維持運轉。 薩爾在宣告中強調，英以在安全和情報領域的合作對保護兩國利益具有極其重要的價值。宣告透露，以色列近期曾向英國傳遞了涉及英國皇家空軍費爾福德基地案件的關鍵情報。以方表示，不希望兩國關係進一步升級，但重申以色列的剋制時代已經結束，任何針對以色列採取敵對行動的國家“都將為此付出代價”。（CCTV國際時訊）

【法國債券遭拋售之後，大型投資者在歐元區債市逢低吸籌】本月法國國債拋售潮蔓延至整個歐元區市場，吸引大型投資者在遭到重挫的資產中逢低佈局，包括義大利國債與企業債。投資者押注，市場對風險傳染的擔憂有些過度。法國國債的這波拋售，推動該國10年期基準國債收益率本月觸及近25年高點，逼近5%。市場愈發擔憂其3.5萬億歐元債務規模的可持續性。衡量投資者恐慌情緒的關鍵指標——法國10年期國債相對德國10年期國債（德債）的利差，已經擴大至1.4個百分點，較月初上漲約三分之二。市場擔憂法國債務危機可能帶來連鎖衝擊，歐元區其他國家國債利差同步走闊，義大利國債利差升至1.1個百分點以上。多家大型資管機構表示，他們已經入場抄底此次拋售中被連帶下跌的各類債券，其判斷是歐元區不會重演十多年前歐元區債務危機那樣的經濟崩盤。安本投資基金經理亞歷克斯・埃弗裡特稱：“這不是21世紀10年代初危機的重演。儘管近期波動劇烈，如今歐洲國債市場擁有堅實得多的制度支撐與市場信心。”他表示，已經新建頭寸押注義大利國債表現將跑贏德國國債。

ASX 200 Extends Slide Amid U.S. Strike Concerns, Price Pressures — Australia's ASX 200 fell 67 points, or 0.8%, to finish at 8,661 on Thursday, marking a second straight session of decline and nearing a one-week low. Sentiment weakened after U.S. stock futures slipped as President Donald Trump ruled out a deal with Iran, with reports suggesting the U.S. military is preparing possible strikes ahead of the midterms. Domestically, October inflation expectations climbed to a four-month high, underscoring persistent price pressures from elevated global energy costs even as the Reserve Bank continues its policy tightening. Fortescue dipped 1.4% amid a standoff with China Mineral Resources Group over long-term iron ore contracts. Other notable laggards included Lottery Corp. (-3.8%), Xero (-3.0%), and Endeavor Group (-1.7%).

### 2026-10-08 14:15:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Imports #Importance 1

German Imports Rebound in August — Germany’s imports rose 0.9% month-on-month to EUR 118.1 billion in August 2026, below market expectations of a 2.8% gain but rebounding from a downwardly revised 5.5% decline in the previous month. Imports from third countries increased 2.2% from a month earlier to EUR 58.8 billion, driven mainly by an 11% rise in purchases from China, while imports from the US fell 0.5% and those from the UK declined 3.8%. Meanwhile, imports from EU member states fell 0.4% to EUR 59.3 billion, dragged down by declines in imports from both eurozone countries, which fell 0.3%, and EU countries outside the eurozone, which dropped 0.7%. On a yearly basis, total imports grew 6%, accelerating from an upwardly revised 3.2% increase in the previous month. In the first eight months of the year, Germany’s total imports reached EUR 946.7 billion, up 4.4% from the corresponding period a year earlier.

### 2026-10-08 14:12:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lithuania #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Lithuania Inflation Rate Accelerates Further in September — Lithuania’s annual inflation rate rose to 6.7% year-on-year in September 2026 from 6.1% in August, marking the highest reading since July 2023. The acceleration was mainly driven by faster price growth for transport (20.2% vs 15.0% in August) and housing, water, electricity, gas and other fuels (15.9% vs 15.8%). Costs also increased for alcoholic beverages, tobacco and narcotics (7.3% vs 7.1%), health (5.7% vs 4.9%), and insurance and financial services (7.3% vs 5.9%). Meanwhile, inflation eased for furnishings, household equipment and maintenance (1.7% vs 2.8%), recreation, sport and culture (5.1% vs 6.2%), education (3.8% vs 5.2%), and information and communication (1.8% vs 2.1%). Deflation in food and non-alcoholic beverages narrowed (-1.6% vs -2.0%), while clothing and footwear prices fell 0.7% after rising 0.8% in August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.0% in September, rebounding from a 0.1% decrease in the previous month.

German Exports Drop for 2nd Month — Germany’s exports fell 0.8% month-on-month to €137.6 billion in August 2026, following a downwardly revised 0.5% drop in the previous month and against market expectations for a 0.8% increase. This marked the second consecutive monthly decline in exports, driven by lower shipments to EU member states (-0.6% to €77.5 billion), particularly to non-euro area countries (-3.5% to €23.2 billion), while exports to the euro area increased (0.8% to €54.3 billion). Shipments to third countries also dropped (-1.1% to €60.0 billion), primarily due to weaker demand from the US (-6.3%), which offset increases in exports to China (4.7%) and the UK (16.7%). On a yearly basis, Germany’s exports grew 6.2% in August. For the January-August period, the country's exports were 4.4% higher than in the same period a year ago, totaling €1,092.7 billion.

### 2026-10-08 14:09:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Balance of Trade #Importance 2

German Trade Surplus Narrows in August — Germany’s trade surplus narrowed to €19.5 billion in August 2026 from a revised two-and-a-half-year high of €21.6 billion in July, but came slightly above expectations of €19 billion, as exports declined while imports increased. Exports fell 0.8% month-on-month to €137.6 billion, with shipments to EU countries down 0.6% and those to third countries falling 1.1%. Within third countries, exports to the US dropped 6.3%, while shipments to China rose 4.7% and those to the UK jumped 16.7%. Meanwhile, imports increased 0.9% to €118.1 billion, with imports from EU countries down 0.4% but those from third countries rising 2.2%. Imports from China surged 11%, while those from the US fell 0.5% and from the UK declined 3.8%. On an annual basis, exports rose 6.2% and imports increased 5.5%. From January to August, Germany’s trade surplus widened to €146.0 billion, compared with €140.4 billion in the corresponding period last year.

### 2026-10-08 14:07:09  #觀點

【高盛預警：柴油價格或將維持高位至2027年】近日，高盛警告稱，受各國煉油產能受限，疊加各國政府與企業補庫帶動需求回暖影響，柴油價格或將在2027年年底前維持高位。高盛亞太自然資源研究聯席主管尼基爾·班達裡（Nikhil Bhandari）公開表示：“我們需要把成品油價格維持在足夠高位，在明年持續形成一定程度的需求抑制。”該行認為，在當前煉油產能持續受限的背景下，全球柴油價格必須保持高位，才能防止回暖的需求超出煉油廠的產能負荷。儘管該行預計，隨著近期霍爾木茲海峽原油運輸有望逐步恢復正常，布倫特原油價格將企穩在每桶80美元附近，但煉油產能疲弱，將持續限制柴油價格。高盛預測，2027年全球柴油與航空煤油裂解價差（即成品油相對原油的溢價）均值將高於每桶40美元，是過去常規水平（約每桶20美元）的兩倍以上。班達裡預計：“如果明年出現需求反彈，全球煉油體系或將達到過去二十年來最高的開工率。”

Rupiah Lingers Near IDR 17,900 as Dollar Stays Strong — The Indonesian rupiah hovered near IDR 17,900 per U.S. dollar on Thursday, remaining in a tight range in recent sessions as the dollar index held near an 18-month high after the FOMC minutes reinforced a hawkish tone among Fed policymakers amid persistent price pressures. Middle East tensions also kept sentiment cautious, with potential disruptions to energy flows raising concerns over oil prices and Indonesia’s import costs. Meanwhile, domestic fundamentals remained in focus. Fresh data showed consumer confidence edged lower from August’s three-month high, reflecting concerns over elevated food prices and higher non-subsidised fuel costs. Headline inflation hit a three-month high in September, while August imports grew much faster than exports, though the trade surplus stayed stronger than expected. Forex reserves held relatively steady in September, near a five-month peak, but continued rupiah support by Bank Indonesia could put pressure on reserves amid uncertain portfolio inflows.

Indonesia Consumer Morale Eases in September — Indonesia’s consumer confidence index declined to 118.1 in September 2026 from August's three-month high of 118.5. The decline was largely due to lower assessments of current economic conditions, which fell by 1.9 points to a 12-month low of 107.5. Meanwhile, perceptions of job availability over the past six months dropped by 3.0 points to 101.1. At the same time, expectations for business activity decreased by 0.6 points to 121.5. By contrast, sentiment toward durable goods purchases increased by 0.6 points to 106.7. Meanwhile, expectations for income over the next six months climbed by 2.0 points to 136.9. On the other hand, expectations regarding job availability also rose by 2.0 points to 127.7.

### 2026-10-08 13:19:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Finnish Industrial Output Growth Slows in August — Industrial production in Finland rose by 0.8% year-on-year in August 2026, slowing from a slightly upwardly revised 4.6% gain in the previous month. This marked the softest growth in industrial activity since May, driven by a sharp 15.8% decline (vs -12.8% in July) in mining production. Output growth also softened in the manufacturing sector (0.3% vs 5.4%) as lower activity in printing and reproduction of recorded media (-13.2% vs -8.6%), chemical industry (-9.0% vs 0.8%), and manufacture of furniture (-17.5% vs -16.6%) outweighed increased output in the food industry (3.6% vs -1.2%) and textile, clothing and leather industry (2.3% vs -2.5%). On the other hand, production increased at a faster pace for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (7.5% vs 3.1%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial activity decreased by 1.3% in August, slipping further from a 1.1% loss in the preceding period.

Japan Services Sentiment Rises to 7-Month High — Japan’s services sector sentiment index rose to 47.0 in September 2026 from 46.4 in the previous month, reaching its highest level since February and exceeding market expectations of 46.8. The reading marked the fifth consecutive month of improvement, supported by stronger sentiment among household-related businesses, particularly in the services sector, despite weaker housing-related indicators. Corporate confidence, however, eased, weighed down by the manufacturing sector, while labor market conditions continued to improve. Meanwhile, the economic outlook index, which gauges business expectations for the months ahead, fell to 47.4 from August’s six-month high of 48.3, marking its first decline in five months. The deterioration reflected growing concerns over the impact of natural disasters and uncertainty surrounding conditions in the Middle East, although the broader economy continued to show signs of recovery.

NZX 50 Rebounds, Closes 0.1% Higher. — The NZX 50 climbed 8 points, or 0.1%, to close at 13,692 on Thursday, halting declines from the previous session and morning trade, mainly buoyed by gains in communication services, consumer staples, and utilities. However, declines in financials, tech, and consumer discretionary capped the gain. A downbeat session on Wall Street overnight due to higher Treasury yields also limited the rise. Caution rose after the FOMC minutes suggested the Fed is likely to raise rates again this year. Rising oil prices also limited the gain, as they raised concerns over inflation and interest rate hikes. Last month, the Reserve Bank of New Zealand raised its cash rate by 25 bps to 2.75%, delivering a second straight hike to bring inflation back to the 2% midpoint target. Among the top movers were Australian Foundation (1.5%), A2 Milk (1.4%), F&C Investment (1.4%), AFT Pharmaceuticals (1.3%), Contact Energy (1.3%), South Port NZ (1.2%), Henderson Far East Income (1.0%), and Fisher & Paykel (0.9%).

Sensex Extends Losses to Second Day — India’s BSE Sensex fell about 0.5% to 72,294 on Thursday, extending losses from the last session as investors weighed the Reserve Bank of India’s surprise rate hike and a renewed rise in oil prices. The RBI raised its repo rate by 25 bps to 5.5% on Wednesday, its first hike in nearly four years, adopting a more hawkish stance and raising concerns over growth. Adding to pressure on Indian equities, Brent crude climbed above $100 a barrel, heightening concerns over inflation and the country’s import bill, while the rupee also weakened 0.4% to 96.775 per dollar, near its record low of 96.96. Meanwhile, all sectors declined except information technology stocks, which bucked the broader decline, with the IT index rising about 1.2%, led by TCS (+2.4%) ahead of its September-quarter results, due later on Thursday and set to mark the start of the earnings season for major Indian companies. ### 2026-10-08 13:01:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Currency #Importance 1

South Korean Won Rises to 4-Week High — The South Korean won traded around 1,337 per dollar, reaching a four-week high and approaching its strongest level since October 2024, as strong external balances boosted the currency. South Korea posted a current-account surplus of $46.1 billion in August, the second-highest on record, with the goods account alone generating a $46.81 billion surplus. September export data continued to provide a favorable external backdrop, with shipments surging 83.5% year-on-year to a record $120.9 billion and the trade surplus reaching a record $49.85 billion. Thin holiday liquidity also amplified corporate dollar selling, helping the won hold up in the mid-to-high 1,330s. Meanwhile, the US 10-year Treasury yield remained around 5.3% after recently reaching its highest level since 2002, while the dollar index stayed near its strongest level since April 2025. The latest Fed minutes also showed that most officials favored another rate increase by year-end, limiting the won’s gains.

### 2026-10-08 12:59:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Netherlands #Personal Spending #Importance 2

Dutch Household Spending Growth Picks Up in August — Household consumption in the Netherlands increased 2.1% year-on-year in August 2026, picking up from 1.2% in July, and marking the strongest growth in three months. Spending on consumer goods rose 3.1%, led by a 5.5% jump in durable goods, with consumers buying more cars, electrical appliances, and household items. Expenditure on food, beverages and tobacco grew 2.1%, while other goods consumption edged down 0.1%, largely due to lower motor fuel purchases. Spending on services also strengthened, rising 1.4%, supported by higher outlays on transport and communication, medical services, recreation and culture, housing, and hospitality. Services account for well over half of total domestic household consumption expenditure. Meanwhile, consumption conditions became slightly less favorable in September, as manufacturers turned less optimistic about future employment and consumers became more pessimistic about unemployment.

### 2026-10-08 12:30:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Thailand Consumer Mood Falls for 1st Time in 4 Months — The University of the Thai Chamber of Commerce's Consumer Confidence Index declined to 52.1 in September 2026 from August’s six-month high of 53.2. It was the first decline in consumer confidence since May, due to concerns about flooding, a slow economic recovery, and the high cost of living as high oil prices persist amid the ongoing Middle East conflict. Thanavath Phonvichai, the university's president, said that economic damage from the floods is estimated at 22 billion baht to 30 billion baht ($655 million to $893 million) and could remain within that range if conditions start to improve next week. Sentiment could improve later this year if flood risks ease, oil prices fall, tourism picks up, and government stimulus measures gain traction, he added. ### 2026-10-08 10:09:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Malaysia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Malaysian Stocks Slip Further, Hit Weakest Since Nov 2025 — Bursa Malaysia’s KLCI fell 0.6% to 1,602 in early Thursday trading, extending prior losses and hitting its lowest since November 2025. Persistent foreign selling, elevated U.S. Treasury yields, and higher oil prices pressured sentiment. Locally, caution was reinforced by the planned expansion of the KLCI from 30 to 50 constituents in phases beginning December, alongside risks of earnings disappointments amid higher-for-longer costs and supply-chain strains. Still, weakness was capped as traders awaited Budget 2027, to be tabled Friday, with hopes for clarity on fiscal priorities, infrastructure spending, and steps to support consumption. Tech, commercial services, manufacturing, and industrials led sector weakness, offset by strength in healthcare, energy minerals, and retail trade. Banks were among top laggards, including Hong Leong Bank (-2.1%) and CIMB (-1.9%). Other notable decliners included Kellington Group (-2.6%), Sime Darby Property (-1.6%), and Telekom Malaysia (-1.0%).

Dollar Holds Firm on Hawkish FOMC Minutes — The dollar index held above 102 on Thursday, remaining near its strongest level since April 2025 as the latest FOMC minutes pointed to a hawkish stance among policymakers amid persistent inflation risks. Minutes from the Federal Reserve’s September meeting showed that all 19 policymakers supported the September rate hike, while most believed another increase would be appropriate by year-end. Markets broadly expect the Fed to keep policy unchanged this month, while the probability of a December hike currently stands at around 78%. Investors now await the latest weekly US jobless claims data for further insight into the health of the labor market. The greenback also continued to benefit from safe-haven demand as concerns over a potential escalation between the US and Iran, along with ongoing risks to oil flows from the Middle East, kept crude prices elevated and inflationary pressures high.

China Stocks Gain on Return From Golden Week — The Shanghai Composite rose 0.2% to 3,848 while the Shenzhen Component gained 0.6% to 12,960 on Thursday as investors returned from the week-long Golden Week holiday. Investors shrugged off broader Asian market uncertainty following an overnight Wall Street selloff driven by a sharp rise in US Treasury yields. Attention now turns to a series of key Chinese economic releases due next week, including inflation and trade data, for clues on whether Beijing's latest stimulus measures are gaining traction. The recently announced package, which included mortgage subsidies, expanded central bank funding support, and a State Council pledge to introduce "a package of practical and effective additional policies," has so far failed to convince investors that it will be sufficient to revive domestic demand. ### 2026-10-08 09:49:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Car Registrations #Importance 1

Indonesia Car Sales Surge 28.1% in September — New car sales in Indonesia jumped 28.1% year-on-year to 79,450 units in September 2026, though easing from a 32.4% surge in August and marking the sixth consecutive month of expansion, according to data from the Association of Indonesian Automotive Industries (GAIKINDO). The deceleration in growth came as the sharper increases in July and August were partly boosted by the GAIKINDO Indonesia International Auto Show (GIIAS) 2026, held from July 29 to August 9. For the first nine months of the year, sales advanced 21% from the same period a year earlier to 679,020 units, highlighting resilient consumer demand despite inflationary pressures and higher non-subsidized fuel prices. GAIKINDO previously targeted domestic new car sales to reach 850,000 units this year. That figure represents 5.8% growth from the 2025 total of 803,687 units. Meanwhile, retail new car sales grew 27.4% from a year earlier to 81,243 units in September. On a monthly basis, car sales fell 2.7%.

### 2026-10-08 09:47:13  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Holds Near Multi-Week Lows — The Australian dollar held below $0.70, near multi-week lows as the US dollar strengthened following hawkish Federal Reserve meeting minutes. The minutes showed policymakers viewed inflation as the biggest risk to the outlook, with several participants seeing further rate hikes as appropriate and others judging policy as only mildly restrictive. Still, markets remained skeptical of an October Fed hike, pricing just a 19% probability of a quarter-point increase. In Australia, markets are also pricing just a 25% chance of another RBA hike in November, after last week’s increase to 4.60%. The central bank's October 13 minutes will be on focus, followed by September labor data next week, with the unemployment rate closely watched after rising to a five-year high of 4.6% in August. A steady or higher reading could reduce expectations for further tightening, while the September-quarter CPI report later this month will be crucial, with core inflation expected to remain elevated.

### 2026-10-08 09:46:26  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Edge Down — The benchmark KOSPI edged lower to around 6,780 on Thursday, extending losses for a third straight session as elevated global bond yields and inflation concerns weighed on equities. The US 10-year Treasury yield climbed to 5.35%, its highest level since 2002, while Federal Reserve minutes showed most officials favored another rate increase by year-end, keeping the outlook for US borrowing costs elevated and weighing particularly on technology stocks. At the same time, Brent crude remained around $100 a barrel, adding to inflation risks and limiting the scope for monetary easing. Losses were seen in Samsung Electronics (-0.56%), SK Square (-4.99%), Hyundai Motor (-3.13%), Hanwha Aerospace (-5.76%), and Doosan Enerbility (-4.97%). However, Samsung Electronics' record third-quarter operating profit estimate of KRW 107.4 trillion, up 782.5% year-on-year and above market forecasts, was driven by strong AI-related demand, lifting memory chip prices and earnings.

### 2026-10-08 09:27:37  #宏觀

【中國代表團完成國際電信聯盟無線電通訊部門第六研究組會議參會任務】據工信部官網，2026年9月21日至10月2日，國際電信聯盟無線電通訊部門第六研究組（SG6）及其下設WP6A、WP6B和WP6C工作組會議在瑞士日內瓦召開。本次會議是2027年世界無線電通訊大會研究週期SG6及其下設工作組的第六次會議。SG6及其下設工作組主要研究廣播電視相關事宜，包括向公眾提供的視聽、多媒體和資料業務。本次會議重點圍繞多媒體移動廣播、廣播核心網、內容來源追溯、隱私保護機制、六自由度空間音訊、超高畫質和高動態範圍（HDR）電視等技術內容展開深入討論，審議透過了多篇建議書和報告的制修訂。我國提交的3篇移動廣播與媒體處理方面文稿被會議採納，參與的3項廣播電視測試與視聽應用方面報告的制修訂取得新進展。此外，會議還介紹了我國提交的關於應急廣播技術與人工智慧生成影片評價方面的2篇資訊文稿。來自國務院相關部門，研究機構、高校、企業等8家單位的24名代表，以線上線下相結合的方式參會。

### 2026-10-08 09:12:32  #MKT News #601318.SS #Impact bullish #601628.SS #CSI300

【National Health Commission, China State Planner issue plan to expand medical rehabilitation and nursing, push insurance coverage and payment reform】China’s National Health Commission and China State Planner issued an Implementation Plan for Expanding and Enhancing Medical Rehabilitation and Nursing. The plan mandates improving multi-source payment systems and, through normal procedures, bringing rehabilitation and nursing services with proven efficacy and urgent public need into basic medical insurance coverage. It directs medical insurance payment reform: adopt disease-based (per-case) payments for short-stay inpatients receiving rehabilitation and nursing and pilot per-diem (bed-day) payments for long-stay inpatients. The plan also calls for refining special-case single-negotiation and other supporting mechanisms, and encourages commercial insurers to develop rehabilitation/nursing products coordinated with basic medical insurance, long-term care insurance and work-injury insurance.

【National Health Commission and CHINA STATE PLANNER accelerate deployment of brain–computer interfaces, embodied intelligence and bionic‑drive tech in medical rehabilitation】China’s National Health Commission and CHINA STATE PLANNER issued an implementation plan to expand medical rehabilitation and nursing capacity, prioritizing R&D and application of frontier technologies in neurorehabilitation, motor rehabilitation and wound repair. They encourage industry leaders, universities, research institutes and hospitals to form industry‑university‑research‑use consortia to speed commercialization and back R&D on brain–computer interfaces (BCIs), embodied intelligence, bionic drive, smart rehab devices, implantable/interventional devices and new biomaterials. The plan calls for development of TCM‑integrated intelligent rehab nursing products and for qualifying innovative rehab devices and drugs to be entered into priority review/approval channels according to procedure. Top hospitals are urged to pilot and document BCI and related product use and to scale innovations across hospitals, community and home care settings.

今日12大單品的終端零售總價大幅回落，八款產品價格下跌，中腰部酒品跌幅沉重。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10759元，較昨日大幅下跌47元，失守10800元整百關口，並創下8天以來新低。

### 2026-10-08 08:45:15  #國際

【斯洛伐克總理：歐洲石油和燃料供應面臨嚴峻挑戰】當地時間7日，斯洛伐克總理菲佐表示，歐洲目前在石油和燃料供應方面面臨嚴峻挑戰，未來秋冬季歐洲能源市場可能面臨較大壓力。菲佐表示，當前歐洲燃料市場面臨多重因素影響。一方面是伊朗與美國之間的衝突，另一方面歐洲煉油產能正在逐步下降，一些過去能夠出口石油產品的國家，如今反而需要大量進口相關產品。菲佐特別指出，目前歐洲柴油供應面臨的壓力較為突出，這可能進一步推高柴油價格。菲佐表示，解決燃料市場面臨的問題不能僅依靠歐盟成員國各自採取措施。他期待即將舉行的歐盟成員國領導人峰會就相關問題找到解決方案。

### 2026-10-08 08:44:41  #公司

【英偉達支援的澳大利亞資料中心企業 Firmus結束簿記建檔】據知情人士透露，受投資者越來越擔心本次發行可能取消的影響，澳大利亞資料中心企業 Firmus Grid Ltd. 已結束本次遇冷的 IPO 簿記建檔工作。不願具名的知情人士稱，簿記建檔於週四早間在澳大利亞按計劃收官，尚未明確發行價與交易架構。本次交易尚屬非公開階段。Firmus 的代表暫未立即置評。這家獲得英偉達投資的企業計劃募資 55 億美元（含綠鞋期權），是澳大利亞史上規模最大的 IPO 之一。有報道稱，公司向潛在投資者推介的發行價為每股 11 澳元，但未能獲得足夠認購支援，簿記建檔在此背景下結束。該定價對應的估值為 437 億澳元（摺合 304 億美元）。知情人士表示，原本簿記建檔截止日為週五，在前期認購意向遠超發行規模的訊號出現後，提前至週四結束。但知情人士稱，部分潛在投資者態度轉向謹慎，擔憂現有股東或會在公司上市後迅速大量拋售股票。投資者表示，這部分潛在拋壓疊加偏高的定價策略，進一步加劇市場顧慮。

### 2026-10-08 08:26:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Inflation Expectations #Importance 2

Australia Inflation Expectations Hit 4-Month High — Australia’s consumer inflation expectations rose to 5.3% in October 2026, up from 4.9% in the prior two months and reaching their highest level since June. The outlook was reinforced by August data showing trimmed mean CPI remained elevated at 3.6% year-on-year, its highest since September 2024 and above the Reserve Bank of Australia’s 2–3% target range. At its October meeting, the RBA unanimously raised the cash rate by 25bps to 4.6%, its fourth hike this year and lifting borrowing costs to their highest level since 2011. Policymakers said inflation remained elevated, with some upside risks identified in August now materialising. They also noted that strong AI-related demand is driving rapid increases in global prices for technology-related goods. Still, the board said monetary policy was well placed to respond to developments, while remaining focused on its mandate of price stability and full employment.

### 2026-10-08 08:25:47  #公司 #觀點 #央行 #國際

【中金：節後投資者信心有望逐步修復，A股或迎“開門紅”】中金公司研報表示，展望節後，A股或迎來“開門紅”。節前A股在多項外部因素影響下表現偏弱，假期期間海外市場漲多跌少，疊加近期國內經濟資料較平穩，節後三季報有望為市場帶來一定的業績支撐，預計10月投資者信心有望修復，節後A股有望迎來“開門紅”。行業配置上，建議關注兩條主線：1. 景氣成長：AI產業鏈的硬體環節業績普遍高增長，但隨著敘事的變化未來或將面臨分化，部分壁壘不高、產能投放進展較快的環節風險較大，而需求確定性高且產能瓶頸難以緩解的領域有望繼續受益，建議關注光通訊、半導體裝置，以及上游電力瓶頸相關行業。AI產業鏈以外，創新藥（尤其是CXO）、電網裝置整體景氣度較高。2. 週期改善：越來越多領域基本面正在從週期底部回升，建議結合產能週期視角關注供需格局改善的領域，如化工、石化和工程機械等行業，有色金屬各領域基本面較好，但需關注美聯儲緊縮風險對金融屬性的影響。

### 2026-10-08 08:20:07  #市場

【從搶著買到打折賣，越南樓市又“變天”？】最近，越南地產股再次跌入債市爆雷後的“至暗時刻”。截至9月30日，越南VN30指數週跌2.46%，多隻熱門地產股猛烈下挫，跌至2022年以來的最低位。2022年，堪稱越南房地產行業近十年來“最悲壯”的一年——大批地產商債務違約，資金鍊瞬間斷裂，破產、清盤輪番上演，留給購房者的只剩數千個爛尾樓專案。如今，越南地產股再次跌進2022年以來的低位，是否預示著危機正捲土重來？近日，記者實地走訪河內、胡志明市的樓盤和中介機構，發現越南房地產業確實再次回到了相似的歷史場景：信貸收縮，流動性緊張，從貸款利率到債券收益率都在大漲——這是一場對樓盤去化和房企現金流的嚴峻考驗。但和幾年前不同的是，行業經歷過一輪出清，能繼續留在牌桌的都是體格更健壯的大玩家；國際產業轉移還在繼續，外資加速佈局越南，房地產的買盤在擴大；政府親歷過崩盤的風浪，正試圖用行政手段和貨幣政策控制過快的擴張節奏。交易量、去化週期、二手房價格……種種資料都反映出今年越南樓市正在降溫。（21財經）

### 2026-10-08 08:16:54  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Languishes Near 2-Month Low — Silver held below $60 an ounce on Thursday, remaining close to its lowest level since early August as the latest FOMC minutes pointed to the possibility of another US rate hike this year, while elevated oil prices continued to fuel inflation concerns. Minutes from the Federal Reserve’s September meeting showed that all 19 policymakers supported the September rate increase, with most indicating that another hike would likely be appropriate before year-end. Markets broadly expect the Fed to leave rates unchanged this month, while the probability of a December hike currently stands at around 78%. Meanwhile, oil prices advanced following reports that the Trump administration had asked the Pentagon to prepare strike options against Iran that could be carried out before the midterm elections. Risks to shipping through the Strait of Hormuz also remain elevated, even as oil flows from the Middle East are estimated to have recovered to prewar levels.
